- Root-caused from the LIVE session s46-5be8e834 still in the dev server's memory: status showed log_images_count=128, log_images_uploaded=0, and /data showed uploaded_image_ids=[] — yet 17 POST /images batches all returned 200. The store's addImagesToSession was silently rejecting every item. All rejection paths eliminated except the data-URL regex `^data:(image/…)`; the route shape filter had passed (string fileid + string data), store not full, no dupes.
- Verified the mechanism empirically in a real Chromium (agent-browser): readAsDataURL on a TYPELESS blob produces `data:application/octet-stream;base64,…`. The real CryoSmart server serves /api/log_image/ responses with NO Content-Type; the script's client-side check explicitly ALLOWS typeless blobs (b.type === ''), so every upload was an octet-stream data URL the store rejected. The harness mocks always sent Content-Type: image/png — the mock-vs-reality gap that hid the bug (same class as the Task-21 FileReader/Blob mock bug). Also confirmed user sessions s33–s44 in this dev.log window NEVER stored image bytes — the byte pipeline had been silently dead for every real run, and dev.log showed the graph/report falling back direct→proxy (502s) → hidden.
- Server fix (import-session-store.ts): addImagesToSession now accepts data URLs of ANY declared mime and sniffs the REAL image type from the base64 payload's magic bytes (new sniffImageMimeB64: PNG/JPEG/GIF/BMP/WEBP/TIFF/ICO signatures); non-image mimes are stored only when the bytes sniff as an image (text/xml/pdf garbage still rejected). This ALSO rescues STALE v3.10/v3.11 script copies the user still has open in their CryoSmart tab. logImagesUploaded is now recomputed (logImagesUploadedCount) in BOTH addImagesToSession and addLogBatchToSession so the "M/N images" strip counts only bytes for KNOWN log refs — M can never exceed N regardless of arrival order.
- Capture script v3.12 (smart-capture-panel.tsx): fetchImageData no longer uses FileReader — it reads blob.arrayBuffer(), sniffs the MIME from magic bytes (sniffImageMime), falls back to blob.type when image/*, then the ref's own filetype/extension evidence (refMimeHint), and builds the data URL itself via chunked btoa. Uploads are now correct regardless of the server's Content-Type behavior.
- v3.12 asset pipeline: map previews (output_group_images) and card tiles (ui_tile_images) ride the SAME byte pipeline — queueJobAssets(uid) enqueues their plain fileids (path/data/http values excluded), called from every site that marks a job scanned (scan loop AND all 5 loader-calibration sites, which short-circuit the loop — the first harness run caught J40's maps missing because calibration marked it scanned without queueing). enqueueRef dedupes by fileid across log refs + assets. Over the HTTPS preview these refs previously rendered nothing (direct intranet = mixed-content-blocked; proxy = app server can't reach the intranet) — today's dev.log showed the user's browser 502-ing proxy requests for exactly these map fileids.
- E2E session simulator bug found + fixed during verification: my first v312-session.mjs modeled output_group_images keys that did NOT match output_result_groups names, so mapAssets' preview lookup (outputImages[group.name]) found nothing. Verified against the user's REAL s46 data (J30 hetero_refine: ogi keys volume_class_0/1/2 EXACTLY equal group names) and fixed the simulator shape — map cells then rendered.
- Verification: new .harness/v312-harness.mjs models the REAL server (typeless responses + one explicit application/octet-stream + one JPEG-magic file): PASS — 11 log refs, 14 bytes stored server-side (11 log + map J40_vol/J40_half_a + tile J41_tile), J5_decoy (non-lineage) never fetched, stale-script octet POST stored (1) while garbage rejected, /image/<id> serves image/png (and image/jpeg for the sniffed JPEG), log_images_uploaded 11/11. ALL older harnesses PASS unchanged: v311 (11 refs/11 bytes), rerun (fresh + cached re-run), v310, v36; v310-unit + v311-unit PASS. Browser E2E (.harness/v312-session.mjs session s59, ALL bytes posted as octet-stream data URLs — the stale-script shape): auto-trace fires, strip "Captured 4 jobs + 18 log images from 2 jobs (8 with previews)", J2 homo_abinit modal 4/4 images (tile + final-iteration logs), J3 hetero modal 7/8 (8th = deliberate dead ref in its hide chain; xml/txt filtered), report per-job DISTINCT log blocks "Log images (3)/(7)/(2)" + map-grid cells "volume.map_sharp" and "half_map_A" rendering from stored bytes, 13/14 report images loaded (dead ref pending its ~2min sandbox TCP timeout — user-side it 404s in ms then hides), Load Demo regression 10 jobs · report 19/19 images after lazy scroll, 390px mobile no horizontal overflow, fresh page 0 console errors (the earlier 667:8 entries were stale mid-edit HMR), lint 0 problems, tsc src clean. bookmarklet.ts confirmed dead code (BookmarkletPanel not rendered anywhere) — no sync needed.

Stage Summary:
- The "128 captured but nothing displays" bug is FIXED at both ends: the script uploads correctly-typed data URLs no matter what Content-Type the server sends (or doesn't), and the store sniffs+accepts any-mime data URLs whose bytes are real images — which also rescues stale v3.10/v3.11 scripts still open in the user's CryoSmart tabs.
- Map previews and card tiles now upload bytes through the same pipeline — the report's map grid and graph cards render over the HTTPS preview instead of falling back to dead intranet URLs.
- User action: re-copy the capture script from the Smart Capture panel (v3.12 header) and re-run — same tab or fresh tab both work. Even a stale v3.11 re-run now stores bytes thanks to the server-side sniffing.
- The old dev.log evidence (proxy 502 storms for map fileids) confirmed the maps were also silently broken for every prior real run — now covered.
- Remaining backlog unchanged (report-html full review + bilingual decision, nu-refine sharp/half map images, graph parallel-edge offset/compact spacing, bookmarklet-vs-panel script consolidation).

Task ID: 16
Agent: main (Z.ai Code)
Task: Fix "images captured successfully (128 uploaded, 100%) but nothing renders in graph/report; opening the image URL in a new tab works".

Work Log:
- Reproduced with the user's LIVE completed session s60-68c250c9 (46 jobs, 128 refs, 127 byte-uploads still in memory): a fresh page load + fresh trace renders ALL images fine (graph modal 34/34, report 151/152 embedded base64) — proving the render path and image bytes are healthy POST-complete.
- Root-caused via dev.log forensics of the user's actual capture run:
  1. The live progress page's status polls went from 700ms-cadence to sparse, then STOPPED entirely ~70 log lines before POST /complete (background-tab timer throttling froze the poller) → the final /data apply NEVER happened → the page kept rendering the stale mid-capture snapshot.
  2. In that stale snapshot, images whose bytes streamed in after the last apply still carried direct `http://192.168.202.11:8080/api/log_image/…` URLs → mixed-content-blocked inside the HTTPS preview → onError → proxy fallback (10s timeout, 502 — app server cannot reach the intranet) → figure hidden. The "打开" links are top-level navigations, which is exactly why new-tab worked while inline <img> did not.
  3. A page reload mid-capture (preview panel) loaded `/?imported=…` but produced ZERO status polls (tab closed/panel churn) — the token lived only in the URL, so the app state was lost.
  4. During the capture, every progressive data apply restarted the report/modal image prefetch, which ground 10s-timeout proxy fetches for every direct intranet URL (~1300 requests in the user's log) — the "storm" that made the report feel frozen.
- Fixes (all browser-verified with 3 fresh end-to-end capture simulations):
  1. use-imported-metadata.ts: `sleepWithWake()` races the 700ms poll sleep against visibilitychange/focus so a throttled/backgrounded tab polls INSTANTLY when the user looks at it again; the active import token is persisted to localStorage (`cryosmart_import_token_v1`) and a bare `/` load resumes polling from it (silent idle on 404/expired); token cleared on final apply / timeout / stall.
  2. image-embed.ts: `imageToBase64(session, path, { skipDirectCryosmart })` returns null without a request for direct CryoSmart URLs; `prefetchImagesForReport(…, { stagedImport })` drops direct URLs from the prefetch when a staged capture is active (explicit flag covers the refs-only phase BEFORE first bytes land; session-URL presence heuristic covers everything else). Plumbed `stagedImport={importState.token !== null}` from page.tsx → LineagePreviewCard → report prefetch + LineageGraph → NodeDetailModal gallery prefetch. Storm: 12→0 requests in sim.
  3. report-html.ts + lineage-preview-card.tsx: new `webAppOrigin` option absolutizes session-image srcs (`/api/cryosmart/import/session/…/image/…`) so they also render in the blob:-opened report (relative resolution against a blob: opaque path fails); preview card passes `window.location.origin`.
  4. configure-card.tsx: last successful trace {project, startUid} persisted (`cryosmart_last_trace_v1`); restored as the pre-filled Start Job suggestion for the same project after a reload.
- Verification (agent-browser, dev server port 3000):
  - Sim 2 (full live capture, unreachable intranet origin): 0 storm proxy requests (was 12), 6/6 report images embedded+loaded, modal 5/5, final data applied live without reload.
  - Sim 3 (reload-to-bare-`/` MID-capture, the user's exact failure mode): page resumed from localStorage, live progress strip shown, capture completed → page updated LIVE to "Captured 3 jobs + 3 log images…", token auto-cleared, report 3/3 images, 0 hidden.
  - User's real session re-test: fresh trace → modal 34/34, report 151/152 (1 not-uploaded image honestly hidden after fallbacks), lazy images load on scroll.
  - lint clean; no browser console errors; no horizontal overflow at 390px/1440px; footer present.

Stage Summary:
- The "images captured but not rendered" bug was a RENDER-TIMING problem, not a capture problem: the live page froze on a stale snapshot (background-throttled poller missing /complete; reload losing the token), and its not-yet-uploaded images fell back to direct intranet URLs that HTTPS mixed-content blocks (new tab worked because top-level navigation has no mixed-content rule).
- The report/modal embed now never grinds the unreachable intranet proxy during staged captures (was ~1300×10s requests in one user run); session-image bytes are the only embed channel.
- Reload resilience: import token + last-trace persist and resume; expired sessions end silently.

The 26 map failures were `api/log_image/download_result_file/...` proxy fetches that cannot reach the intranet origin (10s abort → 502). Confirmed the hetero/abinit jobs lacking log images are J37/J35/J34/J30/J28/J24/J23-class jobs (only output-group previews in the report refs) — capture-side loader/attribution failures, NOT byte-store failures.
- FIX 1 (the 221 images): proxy-client.ts cryoSmartFetch now fetches session-image URLs same-origin (new branch 0) instead of relaying them to CryoSmart; also branch (3) fetches non-API same-origin paths directly. Exported isSessionImageUrl/isDirectCryosmartUrl helpers.
- FIX 2 (the 26 maps): [...path] proxy route accepts a `timeout` query param (clamped 1s–5min, default 10s); bundle.ts passes 180s for .mrc downloads. probeCryosmartReachable() (one cheap request; 502+"Failed to reach" = unreachable) lets the bundle SKIP the whole map batch + direct-URL image batch after ONE probe instead of N×10s aborts. Every failed/skipped map is written to maps/DOWNLOAD_LINKS.txt with its direct intranet URL; never-uploaded images to images/NOT_UPLOADED_LINKS.txt.
- FIX 3 (report cap): report-html.ts reportMediaBlock LIMIT 12 → 24 per job (heading stays honest "Log images (24 / 26)"); image-embed.ts REPORT_LOG_IMAGE_LIMIT synced 12 → 24. Graph modal was already uncapped.
- FIX 4 (numbered series): new trailingIndexOf + collapseNumberedSeries in lineage.ts — titles/file names ending in a bare 2-4 digit number ("Per particle scale factors 007") collapse to the highest number per base. Applied at REF level (text-first; file-name fallback ONLY when the ref has no title — a numberless title + numbered files is a class gallery, kept intact) and ENTRY level (title only). Guards: 1-digit suffixes (mic0/class_5), bases ending in class/cluster/group/frame/mic/micrograph/exposure/blob/mask (volume_class_10, "for group 12"). Initially also added a file-level collapse inside single entries — REVERTED after the v3.11 regression suite caught it eating class galleries ("Final classes" + J4_final_000/001.png). The capture script mirrors the same PASS 2.5 + guards.
- FIX 5 (hetero/abinit capture robustness, script v3.13): slow loader calls get a 20s second chance (same promise re-awaited after the 1.5s race times out — the systematic "hetero/abinit no log images" failure was a working-but-slow loader); huge-log job types (hetero|abinit|class_3d|variability) get an 8s state-diff window (was 1.3s); deep-scan caps raised (array 2000→20000, node budget 6000→24000, per-object keys 80→200, depth 6→8 — a >2000-entry shared insert_events stream was invisible); scan budget 3→5 min; slow-log rescue 40→90s; byte drain 240→420s; MAX_IMAGE_STORE_CHARS 192MB→288MB.

- Portable export cryosmart-capture/v1: full job metadata + log refs + absolute CryoSmart URLs for every image and map, optional embedded image bytes (self-contained) and optional captured credentials. Import restores it on any instance (also accepts legacy {jobs} JSON).
- Answer to the user's question, now also documented in the README + UI info box: YES — the JSON carries absolute intranet URLs for every image AND map, so any machine with network access to the CryoSmart server can re-download everything from the JSON alone (include credentials when the server requires login); with embed=1 images need no network at all, while maps (multi-MB/GB binaries) are never embedded and always download via their URLs.

  * HIGH F-01 open proxy/SSRF: [...path]/route.ts:20-64 + proxy-image/[fileid]/route.ts:43 — base fully caller-controlled, no host allowlist, redirect:"follow" widens it further; can reach cloud metadata (IMDSv1) / internal services if ever deployed off-intranet.   * HIGH F-02 stored XSS via Content-Type spoofing: import-session-store.ts:332-337 + capture-history.ts:655-660 accept ANY declared image/* mime without sniffing (sniffer only runs when mime is NOT image/*) → image/svg+xml with <script> is stored and re-served same-origin (session image route, cache 5min; history image route, immutable 1y — persistent). No CSP in next.config. Fix: always sniff bytes, reject image/svg+xml (whitelist raster signatures), add CSP/sandbox header on image responses.
  * HIGH F-03 credential disclosure: cryosmart_auth/cryosmart_cookie returned unauthenticated by GET /pending (route.ts:35-37), session /data (data/route.ts:49-51), history GET ([id]/route.ts:63-65) and export?credentials=1 (export/route.ts:40,46); guarded only by 32-bit Math.random tokens (import-session-store.ts:127-134, pending-store.ts:58-63); persisted forever in capture-history/<id>/capture.json.   * MEDIUM F-05 size caps: 512MB import cap reads only the Content-Length header (history/import/route.ts:25-34) → chunked/absent-header requests bypass it entirely; /jobs, /images, /logs and legacy /import have NO cap before req.json() buffers the whole body (App Router has no default body limit). Stage Summary:
- 18/18 in-scope files fully reviewed; 20 findings (0 CRITICAL, 3 HIGH, 7 MEDIUM, 10 LOW) and a verified-safe list to prevent duplicate re-review. The core staged-capture pipeline (script → session → poller → merge → graph/report/history) is architecturally sound; the HIGH items are: a hydration mismatch in download-card's persisted-selections initializer, the capture script's legacy fallback being unreachable/token-mismatched (misleading "Legacy import done" console line while the app times out), and the poller's 700ms full-tree re-render churn.

Work Log:
- Read all 16 modules end-to-end (10,509 lines), plus the cross-file contract surfaces needed to verify suspected bugs: api/cryosmart/[...path]/route.ts (proxy URL reconstruction), import/route.ts, import/session/[token]/{data,complete}/route.ts, history/[id]/{image/[fileid],export}/route.ts, and lineage-preview-card.tsx (iframe sandbox flags).
- EMPIRICALLY VERIFIED two suspected bugs with runnable PoCs before reporting:
  1) XSS in reportImgTag (report-html.ts:1402-1407): buildProxyFallbackUrl() interpolates the RAW fileid into a double-quoted JS string inside the onerror attribute (`this.src="${proxyUrl}"`); escHtml() protects the HTML-attribute context but NOT the JS-string context. A crafted log-image fileid like `x";alert(...);"` (no / ? # needed) makes the onerror handler syntactically valid with attacker JS executing when the (deliberately 404) image fails to load. PoC via new Function(): payload runs, img.src set to the truncated URL. Impact amplified by lineage-preview-card.tsx:387 — the report renders in a srcdoc iframe with sandbox="allow-same-origin allow-scripts", so injected JS runs in the APP origin and can read localStorage `cryosmart.session.v1` (CryoSmart cookie/auth). Vector: unauthenticated POST /api/cryosmart/import, a compromised CryoSmart server, or a malicious shared capture-JSON import. FIX: JSON.stringify() the proxy URL into the handler, or carry it in a data attribute like the bundle branch already does (data-remote-src).
  2) Broken direct-URL image downloads in bundle.ts (fetchOne → cryoSmartBytes → cryoSmartFetch, bundle.ts:300-346 + collectPptImages 516-542): full `http://<origin>/api/log_image/<fileid>` URLs are passed verbatim; the [...path] route re-encodes the embedded origin into the upstream path (`${base}/http%3A//<origin>/api/log_image/...` → upstream 404). PoC simulated the route's segment join: target = `http://192.168.4.3:8080/http%3A//192.168.4.3%3A8080/api/log_image/abc123`. So whenever the app server CAN reach CryoSmart, every "bytes never uploaded" image (and every PPTX direct-URL image) still fails 404 and lands in NOT_UPLOADED_LINKS.txt — the v3.13 E2E only exercised the UNREACHABLE path, which skips this code. image-embed.ts:94-102 already strips the origin correctly — bundle.ts must do the same (or cryoSmartFetch should normalize full URLs in branch 1).
- Other findings (file:line, all verified by re-reading callers):
  MEDIUM report-svg.ts:707 vs bundle.ts:584 — offline SVG references `images/<uid>/selected_classes.png` for the select_2D preview but the ZIP stores it as `images/<uid>/templates_selected.png` (collector + HTML report + PPTX all use templates_selected) → select_2D image always 404 in the bundled SVG. Also a minor sibling: report-svg.ts:652 nameless-micrograph fallback "image" vs collector's "micrograph".
  MEDIUM lineage.ts:1489-1494 — dedupeLogImagesAcrossJobs filters only `imgfiles`, not the v3.11 `files` shape (hetero_refine/homo_abinit); legacy cross-job smear of `files`-shaped entries is not deduped.
  MEDIUM bundle.ts:429-485 — includeFinalResults fires 11 unconditional proxied fetches at the default 10s timeout, NOT gated on probeCryosmartReachable() → up to ~110s of stalls when CryoSmart is unreachable (exactly the grind the v3.13 probe was added to prevent).

  LOW pending-store.ts:44-56 — MAX_ENTRIES eviction compares `++i >= store.size - MAX_ENTRIES` against the SHRINKING store.size, so each gc() deletes only ~half the excess (cap enforced asymptotically); import-session-store's while-loop version is correct.

(Could not reproduce the exact throw in the sandbox — timing-sensitive — but the stack frame pins line 155 setBuildCrashed and this was the ONLY unguarded setState-in-effect in the tree; all other component effects audited: ref-guarded / cancelled-flag / boolean-state / run-once.)

Removed the effect entirely plus both imperative setBuildCrashed calls (handleBuild start + catch); simplified the banner render condition. No setState-in-effect remains → an update loop from this component is structurally impossible. Derived value verified equivalent across all transitions (fresh load false; during build false; success false — result set; failure true — progress non-null, result null, building false; new-build-from-crashed false immediately).
- Stress seeders .harness/v3151-stress-session.mjs + v3151-stress2.mjs: hetero_refine capture with 80 class-grouped log images (v2 uses DISTINCT non-collapsible letter-tagged titles so the numbered-series collapse keeps all 80) → bundle build fires 240+ rapid progress events across report-prefetch/PPTX/images phases.

Task ID: 33
Agent: main (Z.ai Code)
Task: User asked to speed up map packaging ("打包map的时候很慢，可否并行下载？"). Root-cause the bottleneck, parallelize, and push.

Work Log:
- Root-caused THREE stacked bottlenecks (network speed was only part of it):
  1. bundle.ts maps phase downloaded every .mrc SEQUENTIALLY (serial for-loop, 180s timeout each) — N maps cost N × (server read + transfer) with zero overlap.
  2. The /api/cryosmart/[...path] proxy buffered the ENTIRE upstream body (await arrayBuffer()) before forwarding — every map traversed BOTH network legs serially (CryoSmart→server, then server→browser), ~2× per-file latency, plus a full extra copy in server memory per concurrent download.
  3. PPT images (collectPptImages), direct-URL images, and the Final_Result scan were also serial loops (session images were already pooled at 4).
- bundle.ts: added MAP_CONCURRENCY=4 / DIRECT_IMAGE_CONCURRENCY=3 / PPT_IMAGE_CONCURRENCY=4 / FINAL_RESULT_CONCURRENCY=4 constants; maps phase now runs through pooledMap with the old "unreachable mid-download → skip queue" semantics preserved via a shared unreachableNow flag checked at worker pull-time (first connection-level failure flips it once — sync section, no race; in-flight requests still complete and keep their bytes); direct images → pooledMap(3); collectPptImages → pooledMap(4); Final_Result scan → pooledMap(4) with a post-sort for deterministic ZIP order.
- [...path]/route.ts + proxy-image route: replaced arrayBuffer() buffering with straight-through ReadableStream passthrough (upstream.body ?? null). Connection-level failures still reject fetch() BEFORE headers → the 502 "Failed to reach" contract used by probeCryosmartReachable() and the mid-run unreachable detection is unchanged; mid-body aborts now surface as network errors in the caller, which every consumer already treats as per-item failure.
- New harness .harness/v316-parallel-maps.mjs (fake CryoSmart upstream on :3999 with 120ms delay + concurrency stats, run against the LIVE dev-server proxy):
  Run 1 happy path — 12 maps: all bundled, maxInFlight=4 (parallel proof), upstream busy 726ms vs 1440ms serial baseline, bytes byte-exact through the streaming proxy (pattern compare), zero warnings, no DOWNLOAD_LINKS.txt.
  Run 2 mid-run death (502 "failed to reach" after 2 successes) — arrivals stop at 6 (no grinding), 2 pre-death successes bundled, 10 links recorded, exactly ONE "became unreachable" warning.
  (First harness run had 2 harness-logic bugs — death check judged by completion-time global counter instead of arrival slot, and the maps/ filter counted DOWNLOAD_LINKS.txt as a bundled map — both fixed; app code unchanged.)
- Browser gold-standard E2E (agent-browser + .harness/v316-upstream-daemon.mjs + v316-browser-prep.mjs): restored staged session ?imported=s58… → checked Map/MRC + Final results → Trace Lineage → Build & download ZIP → "Bundle ready CryoSmart_PX7_J4_lineage.zip · 18 files" with ZERO console/page errors; upstream log shows max=4 concurrent requests from the real browser path. Test history entry deleted afterwards.
- Regression: lint 0/0; tsc --noEmit 0 errors in src/ (skills/ errors pre-existing); v314-regression 24/24; v315-regression 35/35; v313-bundle 16/16; v36-harness capture E2E PASS.

Stage Summary:
- Answer to the user: it was NOT purely network-bound — serial downloads + the double-network-leg buffering proxy dominated. Now 4 concurrent map downloads + streamed proxy (both legs overlap) give roughly 4–8× aggregate speedup on large map sets; if it is STILL slow after this, the remaining limit is genuine bandwidth/CryoSmart read throughput.
- Mid-run unreachability keeps the fail-fast contract (no return to the "minutes of 502 grinding" bug).
- Artifacts: src/lib/cryosmart/bundle.ts (pooledMap everywhere + concurrency constants), src/app/api/cryosmart/[...path]/route.ts + proxy-image/[fileid]/route.ts (streaming passthrough), .harness/v316-parallel-maps.mjs + v316-upstream-daemon.mjs + v316-browser-prep.mjs (E2E verification).

Task ID: 34
Agent: main (Z.ai Code)
Task: User reported the live-capture strip sometimes gets stuck ("Loaded 46 jobs — uploading image previews 263/268… 100% · 24/24 jobs scanned · 268 images captured · 263 ready") with NO way to manually stop it.

Work Log:
- Root-caused WHY the poller never gives up on a stuck upload: the stall fingerprint includes `updated_at`, which the capture script's ?hb=1 heartbeat keeps bumping every ~3s — a live-but-stuck script (hung /images POST, browser-frozen capture tab, or a script whose final /complete POST failed 3× and exited) keeps the session "alive" while the COUNTERS are frozen, so the 10-min dead-tab stall timeout never fires. The strip spun forever with no escape hatch.
- use-imported-metadata.ts:
  - New progress-only fingerprint (status/log_jobs_done/log_jobs_total/log_images_count/log_images_uploaded/note/log_request.revision — NO updated_at) tracked alongside the full sig. When the scan is finished but the byte upload is incomplete and the progress fingerprint stays frozen > UPLOAD_STALL_HINT_MS (2 min), state gets uploadStalled=true. Deliberately a HINT, never an auto-abort: a healthy capture's 3-min re-trace grace window + slow byte-drain tail can legitimately look frozen; the user decides via Stop.
  - New stable stopImport() callback (reads token/endJobUid/startedAt via a ref mirror): fetches the final cumulative /data snapshot, applies it via onLoaded, ends the polling state with an honest "Stopped waiting — kept N jobs + M log images (U with previews)… re-run Smart Capture to fetch the rest" message, and clears the persisted import token. If /data is unreadable (expired session) it still stops with an error message instead of spinning forever. The capture script itself is untouched — its eventual /complete persists to Capture History for later restore.
  - Hook return extended: `{ ...state, stop: stopImport }` (page.tsx is the only consumer).
  - applyState bailout comparison now includes uploadStalled.
- lineage-preview-card.tsx ImportProgressStrip:
  - polling state renders a "Stop & keep data" button (amber-emphasized when stalled; icon-only "Stop" on xs screens) with a tooltip explaining the script itself keeps running.
  - amber "no progress" pulsing badge when stalled.
  - Props threaded through both strip call sites; new onStopImport card prop.
- page.tsx wires onStopImport={polling ? importState.stop : undefined} + uploadStalled into importInfo.
- Browser E2E (.harness/v3161-stuck-prep.mjs + agent-browser): crafted the EXACT stuck state (4 jobs, 8 refs, only 5 bytes, no /complete — session "collecting_logs" forever) → strip shows "Loaded 4 jobs — uploading image previews 5/8 for the traced lineage… 100% · 4/4 jobs scanned · 8 images captured · 5 ready" with the Stop button; after 2 min the amber "no progress" badge appears; clicking Stop flips the strip to "Stopped waiting — kept 4 jobs + 8 log images from 4 jobs (5 with previews)…"; the graph (J1→J2→J3→J4 → target J4), trace, and Build & download ZIP all still work; ZERO console/page errors.
- Regression: lint 0/0; tsc --noEmit 0 errors in src/; v36-harness full capture E2E PASS (normal completion unaffected); v314 24/24; v315 35/35; v313-bundle 16/16; v316-parallel-maps 11/11.

Stage Summary:
- The stuck capture now has TWO escape hatches: an always-available Stop button (manual, immediate, data-preserving) and an honest amber "no progress" badge after 2 min of frozen counters.
- Auto-abort deliberately NOT implemented (would race the script's legit 3-min re-trace grace window); the 10-min dead-tab stall rescue stays as the automatic backstop.
- Artifacts: use-imported-metadata.ts (progressSig tracking + stopImport), lineage-preview-card.tsx (Stop button + stall badge), page.tsx (wiring), .harness/v3161-stuck-prep.mjs (stuck-state seeder).

- Root-caused THREE stacked problems behind the stuck-at-0% maps phase:
  1. NO browser-side timeout: cryoSmartBytes passed timeoutMs only as a proxy query param; the browser-side resp.arrayBuffer() had no signal. Since v3.16 the proxy STREAMS the body, so the proxy's upstream AbortSignal.timeout no longer bounds the BROWSER leg — any leg that stalls without closing the socket (wedged CryoSmart after headers, half-open gateway/dev-server connection) hangs the map fetch FOREVER → pooledMap never drains → 0% forever with no escape hatch.
  2. Zero liveness: maps progress ticked only on COMPLETION — the first minutes of a healthy 4-way large-map download showed an unchanged "0% Collecting maps…", indistinguishable from the hang.
  3. Wedged-but-accepting upstreams burned 180s × N/4 before failing (no mid-body stall detection).
- proxy-client.ts cryoSmartBytes rewritten: owns a local AbortController enforcing (a) absolute cap timeoutMs+15s grace from request START covering headers AND full body, (b) 45s no-data stall watchdog via chunked reader loop (every chunk resets the window — slow-but-flowing never trips), (c) external init.signal (Stop button) merged in. Post-loop guards map surviving aborts to proper errors (runtimes differ on reject-vs-clean-EOF) and Content-Length mismatches throw "truncated". CRITICAL first-draft bug caught by the harness: reader.cancel() as a "nudge" in the stall handler resolved the pending read as CLEAN EOF → every watchdog kill became a "successful" TRUNCATED download (curl probe proved the socket stays open; the cancel was ours). Removed; signal-abort + post-loop guard covers both runtime behaviors.
- bundle.ts: BundleOptions gains signal + mapStallMs; throwIfCancelled() at every phase boundary; maps phase: throttled (400ms) byte-liveness events "Map d/N · X in flight · Y MB received" via onBytes aggregation, stall-failure counting (3 stalls → stopQueue skip, mirroring the 502 unreachable skip, one warning), cancel rethrow in every download pool (maps/images/PPTX/final-results) so pooledMap drains instantly on Stop; opener shows total map count (0/N bar from the start).
- download-card.tsx: amber "Stop build" button while building (abortRef AbortController per build; handleCancel aborts + bumps epoch + clears progress so the derived buildCrashed banner stays OFF); catch treats AbortError as cancel (info toast, not the scary failure toast).
- FIXED LONG-STANDING GLOBAL BUG: layout.tsx mounted the radix <Toaster /> while every component fires sonner's toast.* — ALL toasts app-wide silently no-opped since inception. Swapped to the sonner wrapper (@/components/ui/sonner); verified success + info toasts now render.
- Harness .harness/v317-map-stall.mjs (fake upstream :3998, live dev-server proxy): Run A liveness (6 byte-progress lines, first at +323ms vs first completion +1065ms, all 12 maps byte-exact through chunked streaming); Run B wedged upstream (headers+chunk then silence; mapStallMs=1500): build COMPLETES in 3.2s (was 180s×waves or forever), 2 healthy maps bundled, 10 links, 6 stall warnings + exactly ONE queue-skip warning, arrivals 8/12 (bounded overshoot — second wave in flight before the 3rd RECORDED stall; assert documents the design bound); Run C cancel: AbortError rejection 41ms after abort. 16/16.
- Browser E2E (chunked daemon + staged session via agent-browser): liveness line captured live "Fetching maps | 0% | Map 0/12 · 4 in flight · 0.4 MB received" with bytes visibly accumulating BEFORE any completion (the exact user symptom, now self-explanatory); Stop clicked mid-maps → build resets instantly, toast "Build cancelled — click Build to retry." renders, NO false crash banner; rebuild completes "Bundle ready · 29 files" + "Downloaded …zip (29 files)" toast; zero console/page errors; footer natural-push verified at 390px. (v316-browser-prep only CREATES the session — jobs+complete must be POSTed manually, same known quirk as Task 33.)
- Regression: lint 0/0; tsc --noEmit 0 errors in src/; v316-parallel-maps 11/11 (502 fast-skip contract intact); v314 24/24; v315 35/35; v313-bundle 16/16. Test history entry deleted; user's real entry (s8, origin 192.168.0.99) untouched.
- README: new "Map 下载防卡死与手动停止（v3.17）" section.

Stage Summary:
- "Fetching maps 0%" can no longer hang: every download self-terminates on BOTH legs (absolute cap + 45s no-data watchdog), repeated stalls skip the queue with links recorded, and the user can Stop the build at any moment (data-preserving, no false crash banner).
- Slow vs stuck is now visible in real time via the byte-level heartbeat line.
- The app-wide toast blackout (wrong Toaster mounted) is fixed — all success/error/info toasts render for the first time.
- Artifacts: src/lib/cryosmart/proxy-client.ts (hardened cryoSmartBytes), src/lib/cryosmart/bundle.ts (signal/stallMs/liveness/queue-skip), src/app/components/cryosmart/download-card.tsx (Stop build), src/app/layout.tsx (sonner Toaster), .harness/v317-map-stall.mjs + .harness/v317-browser-daemon.mjs (verification).

Task ID: 36
Agent: main (Z.ai Code)
Task: User hit "Previous build did not finish" during a 66-map bundle build — asked whether it was OOM and whether the ZIP could be written incrementally. Root-cause, implement streaming ZIP output, verify, push.

Work Log:
- Root-caused (user's hypothesis confirmed): it WAS memory, but in the BROWSER TAB, not Node. buildBundle accumulated every file's bytes in a files[] array and then makeZip made two more full-copy concatenation passes (per-entry concatBytes([header,data]) + whole-archive concatBytes(localParts)) — peak heap ≈ 3× archive size. 66 .mrc maps (easily 10+ GB) blew the tab's ~4 GB ceiling → tab died mid-build → reload showed the buildCrashed banner ("Previous build did not finish").
- zip.ts: new StreamingZipWriter — same STORE-only byte layout as makeZip (single dosDateTime stamp for determinism), but add() writes local header + data to a ZipByteSink immediately and retains only name-sized central metadata; finish() writes central dir + EOCD (offset accounting fixed after harness caught cdSize=0 bug — central/EOCD bytes now counted in bytesWritten). A promise-chain mutex serializes concurrent add() calls from the 4-way download pools (interleaved header/data writes would corrupt the archive). abort() marks dead + discards sink output.
- New zip-sink.ts: createBundleSink() — OPFS first (navigator.storage.getDirectory() → fixed-name staging file → createWritable, structural types immune to DOM-lib drift; result() returns the disk-backed File, NOT a heap copy; abort() uses writable.abort() which discards — deliberately no close() fallback since closing COMMITS partial data), silent fallback to a memory sink (chunks array; abort clears). Never throws — any probe failure degrades to memory.
- bundle.ts: buildBundle now opens the sink BEFORE downloading anything and streams every payload the moment it lands (addFile). All 15 files.push sites converted; files[] array + makeZip call removed (makeZip remains for PPTX). Memory-sink byte BUDGET (default 1 GiB, options.memZipBudgetBytes override): maps beyond budget record as links in maps/DOWNLOAD_LINKS.txt + flip stopQueue (no wasted downloads); final .mrc results skip with warning; images degrade to NOT_UPLOADED_LINKS; reports/HTML always written. Sink write errors are fatal (wrapped "ZIP sink write failed:"). try/catch wrapper aborts the writer on ANY throw (no dangling OPFS writable/lock). BundleResult gains zipBytes; progress "zip" phase now "Finalizing ZIP · N files · X MB…". downloadBlob revoke bumped 5s→30s for multi-GB blobs.
- download-card.tsx: handleBuild opens the sink at click time and passes it in; new sinkKind state renders a mode line in the progress box — teal "Streaming ZIP to browser disk storage — large bundles no longer exhaust memory" (OPFS) or amber in-memory-fallback hint; success toast now includes real size + "· streamed to disk"; banner copy updated (streams now; reload still loses the build; check disk space if it recurs). Cancel/finally reset sinkKind.
- Harness .harness/v318-stream-zip.mjs (22/22): A) frozen-clock BYTE-IDENTICAL vs makeZip over mixed string/binary/empty/unicode files (full array compare + EOCD walk + zero-filled CRC); B) 40 fully-concurrent add() calls parsed by an independent EOCD→central→local reader (the mutex corruption test — every entry name/size/CRC/data verified); C) abort semantics (add/finish throw, sink discarded); E) createBundleSink degrades to memory under Bun; D) LIVE integration — 12-map staged session via fake upstream :3997: tiny budget (baseline+2 maps) build COMPLETES with the rest as links (linkCount+bundled=12, zipBytes ≤ budget+3-map overshoot), control build with default budget bundles all 12 byte-exact (CRC list).
- Browser E2E (v317 chunked daemon + v318-browser-prep staged PX9 session, agent-browser; daemon+steps in ONE bash call — sandbox kills backgrounded daemons between calls): restored from Capture History → Trace → Build with maps → progress box shows "Map 4/12 · 4 in flight · 1.8 MB received" + the teal "Streaming ZIP to browser disk storage" line (OPFS engaged — the exact memory fix); Stop mid-maps returns cleanly to Build (no crash banner); rebuild completes "Bundle ready CryoSmart_PX9_J4_lineage.zip · 29 files" (identical count to v3.17 baseline → output equivalence) with live "Map 8/12 · 4 in flight · 2.1 MB received"; Re-download (OPFS File) works; ZERO page errors / console errors. Test history entry deleted afterwards.
- Regression: lint 0/0; tsc --noEmit 0 errors in src/; v313-bundle 16/16; v314 24/24; v315 35/35; v316-parallel-maps 11/11; v317-map-stall 16/16; v36-harness capture E2E PASS; v318 22/22. README: new "ZIP 增量（流式）写入（v3.18）" section.
- Known limitation documented: OPFS requires a secure context — plain-http intranet deployments get the memory sink + 1 GB budget degradation path (warning tells the user to serve over HTTPS for full streaming).

Stage Summary:
- Answer to the user: yes, it was the in-memory ZIP blowing up the browser tab (≈3× bundle size peak); the build now writes the ZIP incrementally — OPFS disk streaming in https/localhost browsers (peak heap = a few in-flight map buffers regardless of bundle size), guarded memory fallback elsewhere. A 66-map build now completes instead of crashing, and the banner should not reappear on https deployments.
- StreamingZipWriter output is byte-identical to the old makeZip layout (harness-proven), so nothing downstream changes — same ZIPs, same readers.
- Artifacts: src/lib/cryosmart/zip.ts (StreamingZipWriter), src/lib/cryosmart/zip-sink.ts (new), src/lib/cryosmart/bundle.ts (streaming + budget guard), src/app/components/cryosmart/download-card.tsx (sink wiring + mode line + copy), .harness/v318-stream-zip.mjs + .harness/v318-browser-prep.mjs, README.md.

Verify the status of the three known past issues (66-map OOM, maps-0% hang, upload stuck 263/268). No source files modified (worklog append only).

Work Log:
- Read worklog v3.13→v3.23 entries (last ~400 lines) for context; then read in full: bundle.ts (939), zip.ts (414), zip-sink.ts (135), download-card.tsx, use-imported-metadata.ts (1001), [...path]/route.ts, proxy-image/[fileid]/route.ts, import-session-store.ts, pending-store.ts, capture-history.ts (930), image-embed.ts, proxy-client.ts, share-url.ts, lineage-preview-card.tsx (859); targeted reads: report-html.ts (reportImgTag 1385-1468, scripts 3147-3213, absolutize/buildProxyFallbackUrl 330-368), lineage.ts (collectUpstream/dedupe 1545-1664), report-pptx.ts (1551-1617), lineage-graph.tsx (effects/pan-zoom/export 1039-1300, modal prefetch 2290-2345, tables 2780-2894), smart-capture-panel.tsx (script fetchT 398-421 + React tail), page.tsx wiring, history/data/images/complete/import routes. Empirically demonstrated the 32-bit ZIP field truncation (zipU32(4GiB+1234) decodes as 1234) via a node one-liner.
- KNOWN-ISSUE STATUS: (1) 66-map OOM — FIXED at the architecture level: download-card.tsx:206 opens createBundleSink() (OPFS-first) and passes it into buildBundle (bundle.ts:219-229 streams every entry via StreamingZipWriter; makeZip now only used by report-pptx.ts:1617); writer.abort() on every throw path. (2) maps-0% hang — FIXED: proxy-client.ts cryoSmartBytes (277-425) owns an absolute cap (timeoutMs+15s), 45s no-data watchdog, external-abort merge, post-loop abort/truncation guards; bundle.ts maps phase adds byte-liveness events, 3-stall queue skip, cancel rethrow; Stop-build button (download-card 454-463). (3) upload stuck 263/268 — FIXED: progress-only fingerprint stall hint (use-imported-metadata 673-696, 2-min amber badge) + stopImport() (941-997) + Stop button (lineage-preview-card 675-691). Task-30 security fixes verified still in place: onerror XSS closed via data-proxy-src dataset read-back (report-html 1456-1468); raster-only byte sniffing in both stores; crypto-random tokens; null-body 400s on every session route.
- NEW FINDINGS (severity / file:line):
  * CRITICAL zip.ts:236-238/281/288-297 — no ZIP64: >4GiB streamed (OPFS) archives silently corrupt (offsets/sizes truncate mod 2^32; entries-count wraps at 65536). The v3.18 fix makes multi-GB builds COMPLETE and then deliver an unopenable ZIP — silent corruption on exactly the 66-map scenario. makeZip had the same limit but was memory-bounded; the streaming path removes the bound and fitsBudget never applies to the OPFS sink.
  * HIGH bundle.ts:714-735 — Final_Result phase buffers ALL downloaded bytes in finalFiles[] before ANY sink write (maps phase streams per-item at :598); 6 final .mrc maps accumulate simultaneously, fitsBudget runs only after the pool drains → same tab-OOM class on both sink kinds.
  * MEDIUM zip-sink.ts:80-83 + download-card.tsx:262-274 — Stop→immediate-rebuild race: handleCancel re-enables Build before the dying build's writable.abort() lands (abort is delayed through non-abortable phases, see next item) → createWritable InvalidStateError → silent degradation to the 1 GiB memory sink.
  * MEDIUM bundle.ts:300-310 — prefetchImagesForReport is not given options.signal (image-embed ignores it); Stop during the report-prefetch phase leaves zombie work for up to (N/8 × 10s) and widens the OPFS race above.
  * MEDIUM capture-history.ts:523-557 (+importCaptureJson 755-766) — historyImageResponse remote fallback fetches ARBITRARY imported-JSON URLs with the entry's stored Authorization/Cookie forwarded, no cryosmart_origin check; 8MB cap applied only AFTER full arrayBuffer() buffering. New SSRF/credential-exfiltration vector via a maliciously edited capture JSON (distinct from the accepted open-proxy posture).
  * MEDIUM share-url.ts:75-87/147-149 — decodeSummaryFromHash has no inflated-size cap → decompression-bomb share link can OOM the recipient tab before the JSON shape check.
  * LOW bundle.ts:448/585 — memory-budget check runs AFTER a full download (no Content-Length pre-check) → a single multi-GB map still buffers fully on the memory fallback.
  * LOW bundle.ts:137-153 — pooledMap leaves sibling workers running after a rejection (Promise.all semantics): post-crash zombie downloads + late progress events into the crashed card.
  * LOW lineage-graph.tsx:1242-1268 — exportPng has no img.onerror (blob URL leaks + silent no-op when the SVG fails to rasterize); lineage-preview-card.tsx:528-534 revokes the download URL synchronously after a.click() (bundle path deliberately uses 30s).
- VERIFIED-SAFE (no action): pooledMap rejection propagation (no unhandled rejections); StreamingZipWriter promise-chain mutex + abort semantics (adds awaited before finish in all real flows); derived buildCrashed (no setState-in-effect remains); import poller effect deps + applyState bail-out; sleepWithWake cleanup; stopImport epoch/ref pattern; OPFS keepExistingData:false truncation; meta.json-last + inFlight lock + import rollback in capture-history; lightbox IIFE (parse-safe, single ensure(), keydown/print/pointer handling, closed-form zoom math); download-all + anchor-scroll scripts; reportImgTag escaping (all interpolations escHtml'd; proxyUrl only via dataset); stores' gc loops; session data/images routes (body??{} + try/catch); collectUpstream BFS bounds; PPTX path (makeZip bounded by embedded image set).
- No files modified except this worklog entry (append-only, per instructions).

Stage Summary:
- All three known user-facing hangs/OOMs are confirmed fixed with code evidence — but the OOM fix has a critical follow-on: the streaming ZIP writer lacks ZIP64, so any bundle past 4 GiB (the exact 66-map case it was built for) is silently corrupt; and the Final_Result phase still buffers everything up-front. Both should be fixed before the next large build. Four MEDIUM items (OPFS stop/rebuild degradation, non-abortable report prefetch, history remote-fetch credential forwarding, share-URL decompression bomb) plus three LOWs. 9 findings total: 1 CRITICAL, 1 HIGH, 4 MEDIUM, 3 LOW.

User request: 7 MB one-shot /data fetch + parse + setState + LineageGraph re-render (590 jobs) reads as "page dead until refresh" in the capture popup; log-image fetching interrupts/sticks easily — add retry + speed it up.

Work Log:
- Phase 2 backend: /data route now serializes the same payload then streams it through a ReadableStream in 256 KiB chunks with exact Content-Length + Content-Encoding: identity (forbids intermediary gzip that would desync received-bytes vs length). JSON shape byte-for-byte identical to before.
- Hook (use-imported-metadata.ts): new exported ApplyProgress {jobs, received, total, phase: download|parse, startedAt} held in a DEDICATED useState (outside ImportState so the poller's dedupe applyState never touches it) + applySeqRef monotonic guard (superseded applies can't clobber newer state). New stable fetchSessionData(token, expectedJobs, {firstApply, isCancelled}) is the ONE place /data is fetched+applied: getReader() byte read with ~4 Hz throttled progress, explicit parse phase after a yieldToPaint() (double-rAF with 150 ms hidden-tab fallback so the "parsing…" state paints before JSON.parse blocks), first-apply toast "Loaded 590 jobs in X.Xs" fired on rAF after onLoaded, badge cleared only after the heavy re-render paints (rAF→setTimeout, 3 s wall-clock fallback for hidden tabs).
- Poll loop: applyStagedData now wraps fetchSessionData with the closure's cancelled flag (a stop raced mid-apply no longer lets a stale snapshot overwrite the newer stop snapshot — latent bug); stall-rescue / progressive / /complete passes all thread sessionStatus.total_jobs as the expected-jobs hint.

Work Log:
- Root cause ① (scrollbar): buildTemplateCss emitted workspaceCss (.flow-pane{…overflow:auto}) BEFORE the base ".pane{…overflow:hidden}" — same specificity, later rule wins → the left Lineage Outline was silently CLIPPED at max-height with NO scrollbar in every v3.17+ skin. Classic's V2 CSS happened to be ordered correctly (.flow-pane after .pane), which is why only the skins were broken. Fix: moved the .pane base rule before workspaceCss → .flow-pane overflow:auto (split) / overflow:visible (focus reading) wins again; webkit thin-scrollbar rules unchanged.
- Root cause ② (missing images): v3.5 lineage scoping left 520 untraced jobs unscanned; their report cards render with no images, reading as "these jobs have no images". Fix — complete-report pass (STEP 3.7 in the capture script): after the traced lineage's images stream, the script POSTs {all:true} to /request-logs (reuses the v3.26 endpoint — denominator extends to the whole project, fetch-all button auto-hides, final summary self-explains), then scans every remaining job via scanLogs(1200000).
- Capture script mechanics: scanLogs(budgetMs) parameterized (default 300s kept); FINISH_NOW now breaks the per-job scan loop at the next job boundary; re-trace grace window 180s → 60s (a late re-trace only affects ORDER — coverage comes from the rest pass; reset inside the loop updated too); drainImageUploads ceiling 420s → 600s; completion console summary now reports the ACTUAL scanned count (scannedCount of ALL_UIDS) instead of knownRequested.length, with an honest note when the budget cut it short.
- App-side messages (use-imported-metadata.ts): waitingForTrace wording → "(its log images are fetched first; every remaining job follows)"; lineageNote drops " for the traced lineage" once the request covers total_jobs; scanDone-all message → "the script is wrapping up…" (the all-ready state is now transient before the denominator widens); final summary gains wholeProject wording — "from W of the N jobs"/"N of the jobs have no readable log images" (no "traced" prefix) when the request covers the whole project, keeping the legacy "traced" wording for early-stopped captures.
- Panel/strip copy: smart-capture-panel step-3 description → "log images stream in for the traced lineage first, then for every remaining job — so the report carries every image that exists"; fetch-all tooltip rewritten (the button now merges jobs into the FIRST pass — the script scans the rest by default anyway). Version markers → v3.27 (script header + site-chrome banner).
- Harnesses: v319-report-templates.mjs +23 checks (241 → 264) — per-template cascade order assertions (paneBase before .flow-pane sticky/reading rule, scrollbar styling shipped, all 8 templates incl. classic); NEW v327-complete-report.mjs (34 checks) — Part A script structure (widening POST before the rest scan, budgetMs param, FINISH_NOW break placement, 60s grace / 600s drain, old constants gone, panel copy) + Part B live REST flow against the dev server (script's exact sequence: session → /jobs → trace {jobs:[2]} → /logs 2 → {all:true} → /logs 4 → /complete; denominator 2→6, done follows, revision bumps, untraced=0, whole-project final message renders verbatim). Task ID: 7 (v3.28)
Agent: main (Z.ai Code)
Task: User report: "如果 log image 缺失，应该用 output group image 代替。看之前的 log 是从 72 个 job 中抓取日志和图片，但是只有 40 多个完成了，有 30 多个没有进行 —— 这个才是主要问题，需要彻底解决" (+ push to GitHub after fixing).

Work Log:
- Root cause ① (the "30-some jobs never ran" — MAIN problem, code-level in smart-capture-panel.tsx): the FIRST scan pass over the traced lineage carried a FLAT wall-clock budget `BUDGET_MS = budgetMs || 300000` (5 min). Every job is individually bounded by its own timeouts (loader second chance 20s + state-diff window 1.3/8s + HTTP probe 8 paths × 15s + flush retries), but the flat cap fired BELOW the natural bound of the scan: 72 traced jobs × ~7.3s/job ≈ 5.3 min > 5 min → the loop broke DETERMINISTICALLY at 41/72, leaving 31 jobs never scanned ("只有 40 多个完成了，有 30 多个没有进行") — matching the user's two runs producing identical counts. v3.27's rest pass would eventually cover them, but it carried its own flat 20-min ceiling (551 jobs × 2.2s+ = the same class of hazard).
- Fix ①: both scan budgets now SCALE with the job count — `BUDGET_MS = budgetMs || Math.max(300000, pending.length * 150000)` and rest pass `scanLogs(Math.max(1200000, rest3.length * 150000))`. 150s/job ≥ any single job's worst-case path, so the scaled ceiling can never fire on a scan whose jobs each complete within their own per-job bounds — it only guards pathological servers. The budget-cut console line now says the complete-report pass (or a re-run) picks the jobs up ("nothing is lost").
- Root cause ② (one bad job killed the whole loop): the per-job retrieval body had no try/catch — a throwing job (weird store state / malformed payload / an unguarded helper) escaped to the phase-level catch and every job AFTER it went unscanned. Fix: per-job body wrapped (`var logs2 = null, imgs2 = []; try {…} catch {warn}`) — the failing job records itself as no-log, streams an EMPTY batch (progress count stays exact, slow-log rescue bookkeeping intact), and the scan CONTINUES.
- Fix ③ (user-requested fallback — "log image 缺失时用 output group image 代替"): a job with no log images used to render a completely IMAGELESS report card even though its ui_tile/output_group preview bytes ride the same capture pipeline (v3.12 queueJobAssets) and already sit in node.images. Added `outputPreviewFallbackImages(node)` (shared exported selector in report-html.ts + OUTPUT_PREVIEW_FALLBACK_LIMIT=6) and a new media block in reportMediaBlock: "Output group 预览 (N) — 未捕获到 log 图像，以输出预览代替" (compact grid, imgs-block so markFailed rewrites the (N) count and auto-hides on total failure). Strict dedupe against the other media blocks: import jobs' micrograph block, select_2D's three template tiles, class table rows with mrc previews, and map grids with output-group previews — those images are never double-rendered; jobs whose other blocks render images keep them, jobs whose blocks exist but carry no previews still get the fallback.
- image-embed.ts: prefetch scope mirrors the rendered fallback exactly (imports the shared helper; nodes + start_job collect the fallback assets' url+src when there are no log images) so downloaded/blob reports embed the substituted images too. Scope doc comment updated.
- use-imported-metadata.ts: the final summary's no-log reason now ends "…; the report shows their output-group previews instead" (right where the user reads the numbers). site-chrome banner + script header → v3.28.
- Harnesses: NEW v328-output-fallback.mjs (58 checks — Part A script structure: scaled budgets both passes, flat ceilings gone, per-job try/catch placement, empty-batch flow, v3.28 markers, summary wording; Part B selection-rule matrix: 11 rule cases incl. cap + select_2D name exclusion; Part C rendering: fallback block, log-block precedence, imageMode none, markFailed heading regex; Part D prefetch mirror via data:-URL passthrough incl. the excluded select_2D tile; Part E jobNode real data path: session-URL tile passthrough + canonical bare-fileid URL + logSrc preference; Part F all 8 templates render the fallback block + figures). v327-complete-report.mjs updated (36 checks): rest-pass + default budget assertions now the scaled formulas, per-job isolation checks added. NEW v328-e2e-prep.mjs stages the 6-job chained-lineage session (J1→J2→J3→J4→J5, J6 unconnected; J1–J3 have tile+OG bytes but NO log refs — the user's 31-jobs shape in miniature; J4/J5 have log refs+bytes; first staging attempt lacked parents → trace covered only J5 → fixed with parents chain).
- Browser E2E (agent-browser, live app): staged session → auto-trace → final summary renders verbatim with the fallback note → Report tab shows "9 images embedded" (3 log + 6 fallback) → 新标签页打开 → blob report: J1/J2/J3 cards each render "Output group 预览 (2) — 未捕获到 log 图像，以输出预览代替" with their tile+OG images, J4 "Log images (2)", J5 "Log images (1)", 9 imgs / 8 loaded+1 lazy-decoded / 0 failed, Lineage Outline overflowY:auto regression intact, zero console/page errors, dev.log clean. Screenshot .harness/v328-fallback-report.png. Test history entries (P328E2E ×2, P327 ×1) deleted + browser localStorage cleared afterwards — PX5/PX8 keepers remain.
- Full regression after all changes: v313-unit 13/13, v313-bundle 16/16, v314 24/24, v315 35/35, v316 11/11, v317 16/16, v318 22/22, v319 264/264, v324-zip64 19/19, v324-security 14/14 (12/14 only when run inside the parallel sweep — the known live-httpd port conflict from v3.27, 14/14 standalone), v325-capture-retry 12/12, v326-fetchall 13/13, v327-complete-report 36/36, v328-output-fallback 58/58; lint 0; tsc 0 errors in src/ (only pre-existing skills/ folder errors).

Stage Summary:
- The deterministic 41/72 lineage-scan cut is DEAD: scan budgets now scale with the job count so a healthy scan can never be guillotined mid-list, and a single throwing job can no longer kill the loop — every traced (and rest-pass) job is now actually scanned.
- "Log image 缺失 → output group image 代替" ships end-to-end: report block + 8-template rendering + embed prefetch mirror + final-summary note, deduped against every other image-bearing block so nothing double-renders.

Work Log:
- Root cause (why 0/72 · 0% looks frozen): every strip counter is driven only by /logs and /images batch POSTs, and NONE can stream before the loader-calibration stretch finishes — lazy-job classification (a FULL deep store walk PER pending job = 72 walks), action×shape calibration (each combo ≈ snapshot walk + 1.5s await + 1.4s diff polling ≈ 3s; minutes when many candidate actions), then the 8-path HTTP fallback. On a real 593-job build that is 30–120s of all-zero counters — exactly the user's screenshot.
- Phase reporting (visibility, the user's ask #1): script gains phase(kind, detail) — fire-and-forget POST to a NEW /phase session endpoint, rate-limited by CHANGE ONLY, kind ≤40 chars / detail ≤220 chars; every POST bumps updatedAt so phases double as heartbeats (a healthy calibration can no longer trip the 10-min stall timeout). Phases reported: prepare (cached-log check) → calibrating (names the job + action + arg-shape index, and the parallel HTTP probe) → scan (PER JOB: "scanning 13/72 · J13 (class_3d)", plus the 20s slow-log second-chance wait named) → rescue (90s window) → grace (15s) → rest (complete-report pass) → drain (byte counts, updated only when a byte lands/fails — no 250ms spam).
- Server: import-session-store.ts gains scriptPhase/phaseDetail/phaseAt + setSessionPhase (bumps updatedAt) + sessionProgress returns script_phase/phase_detail/phase_at; NEW route src/app/api/cryosmart/import/session/[token]/phase/route.ts (POST; 400 on missing phase, 404 on dead token, length caps).
- App poller (use-imported-metadata.ts): SessionStatus + ImportProgress carry phase/phaseDetail/phaseAt; while log_jobs_done === 0 the generic "fetching log images 0/N…" MAIN message is replaced by the phase detail (single trailing ellipsis — dedupes a detail that already ends with …); afterwards the generic line resumes and the detail feeds the strip's new activity row. applyState's sameProgress dedupe covers the phase fields; the 10-min stall fingerprint (sig) COUNTS phase posts as liveness while the progress-only fingerprint (upload-stall hint) deliberately IGNORES them — frozen counters still trip the amber badge, phases can never mask a true hang.
- Strip (lineage-preview-card.tsx ImportProgressStrip): new activity row under the message — pulsing dot + phaseDetail (truncating) + ActivityAge liveness label computed from phase_at with useElapsedTick(phaseActive): "· 3s ago" while fresh, amber "· quiet for Nm" after 90s (complements the stall detector). Ticks only while a phase detail is on screen; announce-free (aria-hidden dot, no extra live region). Mobile-safe (truncates, no overflow).
- Speed (the user's ask #2): ① httpLogProbe fires all 8 paths CONCURRENTLY with first-valid-wins early resolve (was a sequential reduce chain — worst case 8 × 15s = 120s PER JOB; with ~500 no-log jobs in the rest pass that was hours of pure waiting; now wall-clock worst 15s, healthy servers overlap 8 RTTs to ~1). ② Lazy classification does ONE deep scan shared by all pending jobs (scanForImageLogArrays once + new deepLogsForIn(all, uid); was 72 full walks). ③ Scan-loop top uses new quickLogsFor (classic map keys + embedded image_logs, NO deep walk) — coverage identical because deep-cached jobs are never lazy and the v3.9 late-chance deep check is retained. ④ Trace-wait poll 3s → 1.2s. ⑤ Re-trace grace window 60s → 15s (grace poll 3s → 1.5s): coverage never depended on it (rest pass + drain-time request polling both cover late Fetch-all/re-trace) — pure 45s saved per capture. Calibration/diff windows deliberately UNTOUCHED (correctness hard-won through v3.2–v3.13).
- Version markers: script header v3.29 + changelog entry, site-chrome banner v3.29, panel step-3 copy now explains the live sub-step activity line.
- Harnesses: NEW v329-phase-reporting.mjs (62 checks — Part A script structure: phase helper + rate-limit + fire-and-forget, all seven phase call sites with their detail shapes, concurrent probe (forEach + won flag + settled-count resolve, reduce gone), one-walk lazy filter, quickLogsFor placement, 1.2s/15s/1.5s polls, version markers; Part B live REST: phase POST → status GET fields, heartbeat bump, 400/404, 220-char truncation, phases coexist with counters through jobs→logs→complete and survive as post-complete diagnostics; Part C app sources: 0/N message override, phase fields in ImportProgress, dual stall-fingerprint semantics, dedupe, activity row + ActivityAge + ticking). v327-complete-report updated (36): grace-window assertions now 15s. v328-output-fallback updated (58): version markers v3.29. NEW v329-e2e-prep.mjs stages a 593-job session whose states mirror the user's screenshot exactly.

Work Log:
- Root cause (three stacked classes, code-level): (a) fetchImageData's fetchT abort timer was CLEARED the moment HEADERS arrived — a server stalling mid-body left r.blob()/arrayBuffer() pending FOREVER: all 8 byte workers frozen, ZERO bytes landed, ZERO failures counted, no phase posts → the strip read "uploading image previews 0/712" frozen until the app's 10-min stall detector fired the hard "Capture stalled — the capture script stopped responding" error (the reported 报错失败). v3.28's scaled scan budgets had already fixed the 41/72 cut (this run reached 72/72 · 712 refs), so the freeze moved to the byte-drain segment. (b) Background-tab throttling/freezing: the capture script runs in the CryoSmart tab the user backgrounds while watching the app tab; Chrome clamps its timers to ~1/min (or freezes the tab outright) — the rescue's 45×2s sleeps could stretch 45+ min with zero heartbeats. (c) The {all:true} widening POST was single-shot AND unchecked (post() resolves parsed JSON even for 4xx) — a silent failure left the denominator at 72 while the rest pass scanned 500+ jobs off the books (Fetch-all button still visible, "for the traced lineage" note stuck).
- Fix ① (end-to-end fetch deadline): fetchImageData rewritten — the AbortController's 30s timer stays ARMED through headers + body read + base64 (clearT only in the final settle handlers); a stalled body aborts at 30s and feeds the normal 3-try retry path (~93s/image worst case). The retry catch was moved INSIDE run() so every recursive attempt re-arms its own catch (a catch outside run() only covered attempt #1 — caught during harness testing).
- Fix ② (dead-endpoint circuit breaker): imgConsecNull/IMG_BREAKER_N=24/imgBreakerTripped — 24 consecutive empty fetches with 0 bytes stored (3 full worker rounds) OR 60 consecutive after partial success trips → imgQueue fail-fasts (skipped counted in imgFailed honestly, in-flight fetches finish), loud phase('drain', …) + console warn; a successful fetch resets the chain. A dead endpoint now ends in ~2-3 min instead of 10+; refs stay captured for a re-run.
- Fix ③ (25s heartbeats in the silent stretches): the drain posts when dmark changes (as v3.29) AND every 25s while frozen — "waiting for the first preview byte — N fetch(es) in flight, none answered yet · quiet for Xs" (0-bytes wording) / "N ok · M still in flight · quiet for Xs" — the incrementing seconds make each POST a fresh heartbeat for the liveness age AND the stall detector. Same cadence in the rescue loop ("N job(s) still awaiting late log delivery — Xs left…").
- Fix ④ (throttle detection + naming): the drain's setTimeout-gap measurement (>8s → one-time phase: "this CryoSmart tab is being throttled by the browser… keep the tab visible for full speed"); the rescue measures sleep drift the same way. 
- Fix ⑤ (tab-title mirror): phase() sets document.title = "CryoSmart capture · <kind>: <detail(80)>" (titles are far less throttled than timers — backgrounded liveness visible in the tab bar); completion sets "CryoSmart capture · complete — N image(s) with bytes · see the web app tab".
- Fix ⑥ (widening retry): {all:true} POST ×3 (1s/3s backoff) with the RESULT checked (pr.ok); failure posts phase('rest', 'could not widen the log request — scanning the remaining jobs anyway (the progress totals may lag behind)') + console names the app's Fetch-all button as the escape hatch.

- Console diagnostics: the zero-bytes warning now names the breaker when it tripped and adds "keep this tab VISIBLE while a capture runs — backgrounded tabs are throttled by the browser"; the drain resolve line notes the breaker-skip.
- Version markers → v3.30 (script header + changelog block + site-chrome banner). README v3.30 section.

Agent: main (Z.ai Code)
Task: User reported (a) massive 404 spam from the capture script probing 8 REST log endpoints per job (VM62:313 GET /api/job/get_job_log?job_uid=J507 404 etc.), (b) CryoSmart itself sometimes freezing during capture, and (c) after the freeze the log-fetch progress stalls and the app shows "Capture stalled — the capture script stopped responding".

Work Log:
- Diagnosed the 404 flood: httpLogProbe() fired 8 concurrent GETs for EVERY job that fell back to it (loader calibration probes, per-job WS-failure fallback at two call sites, complete-report pass). On builds with no REST log API that is 8 × N jobs pointless requests + console 404 lines.
- Identified two freeze drivers toward small intranet backends: (1) the sustained 8-request probe bursts, (2) the slow-log rescue re-trigger loop firing ALL no-log jobs' WebSocket loader calls in one tight synchronous loop (300+ simultaneous WS job-detail requests).
- v3.31 fix 1 — endpoint NEGATIVE MEMORY: httpLogCandidates() gives each path a stable key; LOG_PATH_STATE maps key → 'dead' | 'win'. 404/405/501 marks a route dead for the whole capture (definitive route-absent); transient 5xx and network errors keep it alive. When every path is dead, httpLogProbe resolves null with ZERO requests and prints one explanatory warning.
- v3.31 fix 2 — WINNER memory: the route that delivered logs is remembered and asked FIRST and ALONE on later jobs (1 request per job on HTTP-winning builds); only if it returns nothing do the remaining live paths fire.
- v3.31 fix 3 — circuit breaker: 6 consecutive live-path probes with no result trip logBreakerTripped → HTTP fallback disabled for the capture (WS loader path unaffected). Calibration loop breaks early once all-dead/breaker.
- v3.31 fix 4 — staggered bursts: first-time probe paths fire 120ms apart instead of simultaneously.
- v3.31 fix 5 — PACED slow-log rescue: loader re-trigger now fires in batches of 25 with 400ms pauses (same 90s coverage, no WS spike); rescueEnd declared before use.
- Validation: node --check on the extracted script passes. Behavioral harness (mocked fetchT/looksLikeLogs, extracted real functions): all-404 server → probe 1 fires 8 requests (staggered 120ms) then 0 forever + warning; winning-endpoint server → probe 1 finds winner, probe 2+ fires exactly 1 request; 5xx server → paths stay alive, breaker trips after 6 probes, later probes instant-null. bun run lint clean; dev.log recompiled; agent-browser: page loads, zero console/page errors, Copy Capture Script button works.

Stage Summary:
- The 404 console spam on this user's build drops from thousands of lines to exactly 8 (one staggered calibration burst), the HTTP fallback self-disables for the rest of the capture, the WS rescue no longer spikes the server, and the CryoSmart freeze + "Capture stalled" chain is defused. Script is v3.31; re-copy from the Smart Capture panel.

Task ID: 8 (v3.35)
Agent: main (Z.ai Code)
Task: User report: v3.34 run on the 593-job build — "[CryoSmart] Calibrating on job(s): J606, J602, J586" → every HTTP log probe 404 → "No HTTP log endpoint on this CryoSmart build" → "593/593 jobs scanned · 0 images captured"; user asked to check the git history for how log images were fetched in the past. Root-cause and fix (+ push to GitHub).

Work Log:
- Read the console trace: calibration found NO winning loader, fell to the HTTP fallback, probes 404'd, HTTP fallback disabled → nothing ever loaded logs → 0 images.
- Git-history analysis (v3.2–v3.33): the winning mechanism was the BLIND SHAPE SWEEP — calling pinia store actions with 6 guessed shapes ([uid, {job_uid}, {uid}, [uid], row, {uid, project_uid}]); the pinia wrapper forwards arguments to the original action; getLogsByJob(uid) fired WS insert_events and the deep-scan harvested logsByJob state (this is how 903/320/128 images were captured in past runs).
- THE ROOT CAUSE of v3.34's 0-image regression (proven with a node test against real pinia 2.1.7 in /tmp): pinia store actions are WRAPPERS — String(store.getLogsByJob) is pinia's own "function () { const args = Array.from(arguments); … }"; the original action's parameter list lives in a closure and is UNREADABLE from the store. v3.34's parseFnParams therefore parsed the wrapper, saw "no parameters", and synthCallArgs called every loader with ZERO arguments — no logs could ever load. Its 18/18 harness passed only because it tested raw functions, never a wrapped one.
- v3.35 design: restore the v3.2–v3.33 shape sweep AND kill the pre-v3.34 ServerError popups at the RPC boundary instead of by refusing to call:
  * ensureRpcPatch() wraps every function property of socketStore.socketManager (own + 3 prototype levels, in place) plus sm.ws.send; outside our call windows the wrappers are pure pass-throughs.
  * armRepair(uid) arms a 1.2s window around EACH of our calls (async action bodies fire their RPC a microtask or two after the sync call returns) and sets the repair context (jobUid / projectUid / expUid from the job row).
  * repairUidFields: fills undefined/null/'' uid-family fields (job_uid/job_id/job/uid/id, project_*/pid, experiment_*/workspace_*) from the armed context; undoes value confusions (job uid sitting in the project slot etc.); recursive to depth 3; now returns whether it changed anything.
  * repairArgs: (1) repairs payload objects, (2) positional under-supply — a 2-param action called with 1 arg forwards (job_uid, undefined); when the job-uid string sits in an earlier slot and a later one is undefined, rebuilds the (project_uid, job_uid) pair (the exact order the user's server error proved for get_job_streamlog); otherwise fills the first undefined positional with a held uid.
  * payloadIncomplete + swallow: after repair, any argument object still carrying an EMPTY uid field → the RPC is swallowed (never reaches the server) — the "missing 2 required positional arguments" TypeError can never fire. Verified end-to-end in the harness with a null-project context.
  * completeJobSlotOnConfusion (ws.send level): JSON.stringify drops undefined keys, so a stringified confusion frame shows project_uid=<job uid> with NO job key; ONLY that exact pattern gets both keys completed (an SPA-built correct frame never matches, so no unexpected-kwarg errors).
- All three loader call sites (calibration, per-job retry, paced rescue) go through shapesFor(uid)[winning.shapeIdx] + armRepair(uid) (v3.33 structure restored); removed the v3.34 synth block (parseFnParams/knownArgFor/payloadKeyParamMap/synthCallArgs); winning = {action, shapeIdx, mode} again.
- Console honesty: ensureRpcPatch prints its arming line; the win line appends the repair count; the no-winner diagnostics print "rpc repairs during scan"; the loader-source diagnostic now says the pinia wrapper is EXPECTED (and why v3.34 could never work).
- Version markers: script header v3.35 + full header explanation; kept the v3.34 image-reject-reasons server/script code (harmless, orthogonal).

Stage Summary:
- 0-images regression ELIMINATED: loaders receive guessed shapes again (the historically proven mechanism), now with RPC-boundary argument completion so half-empty payloads never reach the server.
- ServerError popups stay dead — not by refusing to call (v3.34's dead end) but by repairing the outgoing RPC (fills, swaps, positional-pair rebuild, swallow-when-uncompletable).
- SPA's own traffic is never touched: repairs run only inside the 1.2s armed window around our calls and only edit incomplete/misplaced uid fields.
- Harness .harness/v335-rpc-repair.js 26/26 — end-to-end against a REAL require('pinia') store (the v3.34 blind spot): wrapper-forwarded getLogsByJob(uid) reaches the wire (v3.34 sent zero args), the under-supplied streamlog payload is repaired to {project_uid: P222, job_uid: J606}, uncompletable payloads are swallowed, unarmed SPA calls pass byte-identical, ws confusion frames are completed, all three replay sites use winning.shapeIdx. Worker onerror flushes waiters onto main-thread timers; every site falls back to plain setTimeout when Worker/blob is unavailable.

Work Log:
- Read the live-vs-restore paths end-to-end (lineage-preview-card embed effect, use-imported-metadata poll/complete flow, image-embed.ts prefetch, report-html.ts reportImgTag, lineage.ts collectImages, mergeLogImagesIntoRaw, import-session-store, capture-history save/restore).
- Reproduced the SUCCESS path first (sandbox sims): basic staged capture (6 images), heavy 72-job/314-image capture with progressive churn — live embed after /complete worked ("314 images embedded"), blob report fully self-contained. So the failure is data-shape/environment dependent.
- Built the discriminating repro: a REFS-ONLY capture (log-image refs with NO uploaded bytes; cryosmart_origin pointed at a local fake CryoSmart server on :9999 that the app-server proxy CAN reach — mirroring the user's LAN deployment where the proxy works but direct report-context loads fail, the original bug #1).
- ROOT CAUSE PROVEN by the repro: live embed → "Image embedding failed — falling back to remote URLs" (0 embedded — every direct URL skipped); restore of the SAME capture → "9 images embedded" via the app-server proxy. The asymmetry: `stagedImport = importState.token !== null` stays TRUE after completion (token retained in the "loaded" state), so the post-capture embed passes `skipDirectCryosmart: true` forever — but the "bytes are about to arrive" premise is void once the capture finishes. Restore passes stagedImport=false AND (zero bytes ⇒ no session URLs in the urlList) so the old short-circuit `opts.stagedImport || urlList.some(isSessionImageUrl)` didn't fire → the same direct URLs got embedded through the proxy — exactly the slow visible embed progress the user watched on restore.
- Fix 1 (lineage-preview-card.tsx): component-level `captureStreaming = stagedImport && importStatus === "polling"`; the embed effect's defer, the prefetch options (`{ stagedImport: captureStreaming }`), and the LineageGraph prop (modal pre-embed's skipDirectCryosmart) all use it — the skip is valid ONLY while the capture is actively streaming.
- Fix 2 (image-embed.ts): prefetch short-circuit tightened from `opts?.stagedImport || urlList.some(isSessionImageUrl)` to `opts?.stagedImport === true` — the session-URL-presence heuristic also killed direct variants of mixed captures in RESTORED/post-capture data long after any bytes could arrive. Unreachable proxies stay bounded (10s abort + the v3.38 PROXY_DEAD breaker: 6 consecutive fails → skip the tail with the named explanation).
- Fix 3 (report-html.ts): the onerror proxy-fallback URL (`/api/proxy-image/<fileid>?...`) was a bare RELATIVE path — in the blob:/file: report contexts `this.src = this.dataset.proxySrc` resolved against the blob:'s opaque path and always failed, so refs-only images lost their only working fallback exactly where they needed it. absolutizeSessionUrl now covers `/api/proxy-image/` paths too (absolutized with webAppOrigin) — on proxy-reachable deployments unembedded direct images now render through the app-server fallback (it forwards the captured CryoSmart credentials).
- Verification (agent-browser on the live dev server, dev.log cross-checked): refs-only live capture → "9 images embedded" (before the fix: "Image embedding failed — falling back to remote URLs"); blob report = 9/9 data URLs, 0 failed, 0 hidden; dev.log shows the 9 proxied GETs `GET /api/cryosmart/api/log_image/J?_ref_? … 200`. With-bytes regression: s4 reload → "314 images embedded". Restore regression: "9 images embedded". Deferral during an active capture unchanged ("Report images embed automatically once the capture finishes…"); completing that session fires the embed immediately. Unreachable-origin vol_* previews trip the breaker after 6 × 10.1s and skip the tail by design. bun run lint clean; tsc clean for src (pre-existing example/bridge errors only). Zero console/page errors.

Stage Summary:
- Root cause: live-vs-restore asymmetry — `stagedImport` (token !== null) outlives the capture, so the post-capture report embed skipped every direct CryoSmart URL forever ("bytes incoming" premise dead), while restore embedded the same URLs through the app-server proxy.
- Fixed in three layers: gate the skip to the actively-streaming window (captureStreaming), drop the session-URL-presence short-circuit, and absolutize the blob-context proxy fallback.
